Proof `67e62d59c9cecbaf7235ce8bcf2ab807369f0a6235196aa8dbc321c50fb5b9ec`, review `45a7af0785d3efce07c0dba8bbd3c12114494a53b03d971bd76d99255cdde4ae`, constructs an actual semialgebraic inductive certificate for each rational-residue small-loss family already known to be nonattained. The two old rational normals are scaled to integer rows, giving rational monomial potentials. Seven auxiliary variables encode algebraic loss/power-sum constraints and a strict flag; the proof checks the updates at **every** auxiliary state satisfying those inequalities, without assuming it came from a word. A low-pair region is absorbing. Positive denominator margins, the Cauchy update and multiplicative potential inequalities establish induction under both append families. Existential projection supplies an inductive base-state set.

The old corner-uniform estimates justify a terminating search for rational intervals and constants by RCF alone. No logarithmic feasibility oracle is assumed. The certificate excludes every nonordinary point above its rational pair floor at which both normal monomial potentials equal one, including sufficiently small pure Poisson kernels at that rational residue. A supplied finite cover of an entire faithfully compiled target fibre by such rejection regions can be checked by RCF and rejected by intersecting the corresponding invariants. The auxiliary dimension is fixed, but integer monomial exponents and resulting formula degrees vary with the residue. Thus the old necessary unbounded-degree result is respected. ### Positive prefixes, joint hulls and nondefinability

The accepted prefix corollary, proof `bcde94be75ef70340371913a631e42e6d109de74b654fb8079001eb186bbb96f`, review `ac5a0187d82c232b07d9cc26cc848a103a6b650d0729d03d27974d99fda834be`, extends the old forced-boundary mechanism to any fixed positive closure prefix. A transcendental-residue contact forbids a separator in this class; reduced rational residue b/q forces a defining atom of slot degree at least q. The product-hull corollary, proof `4237815f84b50c7674d9a2b3b87a62a1fb6030d1d5cda2bad924898977158f72`, review `256e0d4d75c68c1b3bf42c0a38d8e94a2d8ed9116afc28326364b0ab5e0caa9b`, defines H as the set-theoretic intersection of all one-slot semialgebraic inductive supersets with arbitrary real coefficients. A definition of H would project to an infinite countable definable residue set, contradicting o-minimality. No common residue cutoff or decision at algebraic irrational residues is needed. [Wilkie's primary exposition](https://eprints.maths.manchester.ac.uk/1745/1/Bourbaki_Wilkie.pdf) supplies the parameter-inclusive definition and o-minimality of the real exponential field.

This nondefinability is **not** asserted for the actual source image S, does not identify H with S, and does not exhibit a negative algebraic input lacking a certificate. Coincident rates are included without an exceptional stratum. Two is a sufficient bound, not a one-site impossibility or minimal-feature theorem. ### Prospective practical release gates

All empirical/statistical interpretation remains CONVERGENCE_UNRESOLVED. No further chain batch is silently authorized by this record. These are practical screens, not universal sufficiency guarantees. Probability precision is stricter: Bernoulli MCSE 0.01 can require 2,500 effective draws; a normal-approximation 95% Monte Carlo half-width 0.01 can require 9,604. Nonstationarity prevents a certified runtime or accuracy promise.

The pinned ordinary no-date engine initializes theta/root tau from prior means; no documented explicit continuous-start override was found, and resume restores RNG/state.
